raw)\n\nmerged['_normalized_final'] = merged['Final Disposition'].apply(normalize_final_disp)\n\n# Disposition Mismatch\nplanned_lower = merged['Planned Disposition'].astype(str).str.strip().str.lower()\nfinal_lower = merged['_normalized_final'].fillna('').astype(str).str.strip().str.lower()\n\nmismatch = (merged['Missing Final Event'] == 0) & (final_lower != planned_lower)\nmerged['Disposition Mismatch'] = mismatch.astype(int)\n\nmerged['Total Errors'] = (merged['Missing Final Event'] + merged['Disposition Mismatch']).astype(int)\n\n# Error Summary strings\n\ndef error_summary(row):\n    m = int(row['Missing Final Event'])\n    d = int(row['Disposition Mismatch'])\n    if m == 0 and d == 0:\n        return 'None'\n    if m == 1 and d == 0:\n        return 'Missing Final Event'\n    if m == 0 and d == 1:\n        return 'Disposition Mismatch'\n    return 'Missing Final Event, Disposition Mismatch'\n\nmerged['Error Summary'] = merged.apply(error_summary, axis=1)\n\n# Final Formatted Data columns (12 total)\nformatted_out = merged.loc[:, required_plan_cols + [\n    'Missing Final Event',\n    'Disposition Mismatch',\n    'Total Errors',\n    'Error Summary',\n]].copy()\n\n# Ensure integer dtypes exactly\nfor col in ['Missing Final Event', 'Disposition Mismatch', 'Total Errors']:\n    formatted_out[col] = formatted_out[col].astype(int)\n\n# Step 3: Summary sheet\nsummary = (\n    formatted_out\n    .groupby(['Warehouse', 'Carrier'], as_index=False)\n    .agg({\n        'Missing Final Event': 'sum',\n        'Disposition Mismatch': 'sum',\n        'Total Errors': 'sum'\n    })\n)\nsummary = summary.loc[summary['Total Errors'] > 0].copy()\nsummary = summary.sort_values(['Warehouse', 'Carrier'], ascending=[True, True])\n\nsummary = summary.rename(columns={\n    'Missing Final Event': 'Missing Final Events',\n    'Disposition Mismatch': 'Disposition Mismatches',\n})\n\n# Grand total (use dataset-wide totals, not only filtered rows)\ntotal_missing = int(formatted_out['Missing Final Event'].sum())\ntotal_mismatch = int(formatted_out['Disposition Mismatch'].sum())\ntotal_errors = int(formatted_out['Total Errors'].sum())\n\ngrand_total_row = pd.DataFrame([{\n    'Warehouse': 'Grand Total',\n    'Carrier': '-',\n    'Missing Final Events': total_missing,\n    'Disposition Mismatches': total_mismatch,\n    'Total Errors': total_errors,\n}])\n\nsummary_out = pd.concat([summary, grand_total_row], ignore_index=True)\n\n# Enforce integer columns in Summary\nfor col in ['Missing Final Events', 'Disposition Mismatches', 'Total Errors']:\n    summary_out[col] = summary_out[col].astype(int)\n\n# Step 4: Write the Excel file\nout_xlsx = '/root/Returns_Disposition_Audit.xlsx'\nwith pd.ExcelWriter(out_xlsx, engine='openpyxl') as writer:\n    rawdata.to_excel(writer, index=False, sheet_name='RawData')\n    formatted_out.to_excel(writer, index=False, sheet_name='Formatted Data')\n    summary_out.to_excel(writer, index=False, sheet_name='Summary')\n\nprint(f\"Wrote Excel: {out_xlsx}\")\n\n# Step 5: Create the Word document\n# Find top Return IDs by Total Errors summed across lines\nret_err = (\n    formatted_out\n    .groupby('Return ID', as_index=False)\n    .agg({'Total Errors': 'sum'})\n    .sort_values(['Total Errors', 'Return ID'], ascending=[False, True])\n)\n\n# Always pick top 2 entries (even if totals are zero, but typically nonzero)\ntop_returns = ret_err.head(2)['Return ID'].tolist()\n\nout_docx = '/root/Returns_Disposition_Brief.docx'\ndoc = Document()\n\n# 3-6 sentence executive summary\ntext = (\n    f\"This audit compares planned return dispositions to the latest recorded COMPLETED disposition event for each return line. \"\n    f\"A 'Missing Final Event' indicates that no completed disposition event was recorded for a given return line, while a 'Disposition Mismatch' indicates that the final recorded disposition differs from the planned disposition (after normalizing known disposition aliases). \"\n    f\"In total, we found {total_missing} Missing Final Events and {total_mismatch} Disposition Mismatches, for {total_errors} Total Errors across the dataset. \"\n    f\"Return IDs {top_returns[0]} and {top_returns[1]} have the highest accumulated errors and should be prioritized for investigation and remediation. \"\n    f\"Recommendation: implement an automated daily reconciliation that flags return lines lacking a COMPLETED disposition event within a defined SLA window, and standardize disposition values at the point of capture using the alias mapping to reduce downstream mismatches.\"\n)\n\ndoc.add_paragraph(text)\ndoc.save(out_docx)\n\nprint(f\"Wrote Word: {out_docx}\")\n\n# Step 6: Validation\nprint('\\n=== Validation ===')\n\n# 1) Read back Excel and confirm sheet names\nxlsx_dict = pd.read_excel(out_xlsx, sheet_name=None)\nprint('Excel sheet names:', list(xlsx_dict.keys()))\n\n# 2) Print Formatted Data columns + first few rows\nformatted_read = pd.read_excel(out_xlsx, sheet_name='Formatted Data')\nprint('\\nFormatted Data columns:', formatted_read.columns.tolist())\nprint(formatted_read.head(5))\n\n# 3) Print Summary sheet\nsummary_read = pd.read_excel(out_xlsx, sheet_name='Summary')\nprint('\\nSummary sheet:')\nprint(summary_read)\n\n# 4) Read back Word doc text\nfrom docx import Document as DocReader\nrdoc = DocReader(out_docx)\nfull_text = \"\\n\".join([p.text for p in rdoc.paragraphs if p.text is not None])\nprint('\\nWord document text:')\nprint(full_text)\n\n# 5) Print totals\nprint('\\nTotals:')\nprint('Missing Final Events total:', total_missing)\nprint('Disposition Mismatches total:', total_mismatch)\nprint('Total Errors total:', total_errors)\n\nPY","description":"Generate Returns disposition audit Excel and brief Word doc, then validate Mismatch'\n\nmerged['Error Summary'] = [error_summary(int(m), int(d)) for m,d in zip(merged['Missing Final Event'], merged['Disposition Mismatch'])]\n\nformatted_out = merged.loc[:, required_plan_cols + ['Missing Final Event','Disposition Mismatch','Total Errors','Error Summary']].copy()\nfor col in ['Missing Final Event','Disposition Mismatch','Total Errors']:\n    formatted_out[col] = formatted_out[col].astype(int)\n\n# Summary\nsummary = (formatted_out.groupby(['Warehouse','Carrier'], as_index=False)\n           .agg({'Missing Final Event':'sum','Disposition Mismatch':'sum','Total Errors':'sum'}))\nsummary = summary.loc[summary['Total Errors']>0].sort_values(['Warehouse','Carrier']).copy()\nsummary = summary.rename(columns={'Missing Final Event':'Missing Final Events','Disposition Mismatch':'Disposition Mismatches'})\n\n# Grand totals from full dataset\nmissing_total = int(formatted_out['Missing Final Event'].sum())\nmismatch_total = int(formatted_out['Disposition Mismatch'].sum())\nerrors_total = int(formatted_out['Total Errors'].sum())\n\ngrand_total = pd.DataFrame([{'Warehouse':'Grand Total','Carrier':'-','Missing Final Events':missing_total,'Disposition Mismatches':mismatch_total,'Total Errors':errors_total}])\nsummary_out = pd.concat([summary, grand_total], ignore_index=True)\nfor col in ['Missing Final Events','Disposition Mismatches','Total Errors']:\n    summary_out[col] = summary_out[col].astype(int)\n\nout_xlsx='/root/Returns_Disposition_Audit.xlsx'\nwith pd.ExcelWriter(out_xlsx, engine='openpyxl') as writer:\n    rawdata.to_excel(writer, index=False, sheet_name='RawData')\n    formatted_out.to_excel(writer, index=False, sheet_name='Formatted Data')\n    summary_out.to_excel(writer, index=False, sheet_name='Summary')\n\nprint('Rewrote Excel with ordering column:', ordering_col)\nprint('Totals:', missing_total, mismatch_total,
